Literal Markdown bytes and golden tests do not prove phone/TUI visual behavior: final narrow/wide host rendering and public/source/packed snapshot acceptance remain gated.

The planned payload contains only these fields, plus optional `phase: "stopping"` or the bounded `evidence_error` code under the conditions below:

```json
{
  "kind": "run_snapshot",
  "run": "review",
  "run_instance_id": "<opaque generation>",
  "captured_at": "<ISO timestamp>",
  "elapsed_ms": 250000,
  "status": "running",
  "cwd": "~/work/project",
  "evidence": "~/.pi/agent/tmp/pi-actors/runs/review/context-index.json"
}
```

`cwd` is the captured Run launch working directory, not the coordinator's later directory or a guessed current leaf-command directory. `evidence` points to the readable owned context index defined below, not merely a directory that the agent must scan. Both are curated operational references; prefer readable home-relative paths, preserve authorized exact references when needed, and do not disclose private Skill installation paths, secrets, environment dumps, or unrelated machine-local paths. Operational references must not bypass redaction: export evidence to an authorized readable location rather than revealing private provenance. Paths alone confer no authority.

`elapsed_ms` is nonnegative duration at `captured_at` while active and freezes at confirmed terminal duration after completion. Manual inspection can return any truthful current Run status; the timer admits only currently nonterminal snapshots. `running` describes verified execution state, not health or progress. Unknown identity fails boundedly rather than producing an invented healthy snapshot. If the generation is valid but its index is unavailable, return `evidence: null` with the bounded code `evidence_error: "index_unavailable"`; do not publish a nonexistent path or create exports as an inspection side effect.

### Compact Manual Inspect API

Add a Run-only `status` view and make it the default when `view` is omitted:

```json
{"target":"run:review"}
```

`inspect target=run:review view=status` is equivalent. Compact text is the default; `verbose: true` returns the same bounded snapshot as JSON, not extra logs or a complete raw Run dump in content/details. Snapshot view rejects inapplicable `lines`, `source`, `identity`, and runtime inventory `status` filters instead of silently ignoring them. Other target families still require their existing explicit views. Existing explicit expert Run views remain available in this increment; they are not the ordinary status path and must not be invoked automatically to expand a compact snapshot.

Snapshot inspection is read-only: it cannot reset clocks, change report eligibility, acknowledge a pending automatic diagnostic/completion, signal processes, scan directories, or load role bodies beyond bounded identity/index metadata. The payload is refreshed for the manual call; equality with a timed report means the same schema and authoritative facts at the same observation, not reuse of a stale cached heartbeat. Manual responses remain ordinary tool evidence; latest-only folding applies to the separate automatic diagnostic stream, not to arbitrary tool results.

### Readable Context Index

#### Same-session File Investigation

**Accepted owner decision; explicit verbose runtime inventory now implements a bounded read-side subset.** A2 is a navigation contract, not recovery of an original-producer capability. Kernel shows the owning logical Pi session where to start reading its Run files; the agent investigates selectively through ordinary file tools.

- **Session boundary:** Bind the actual active logical Pi session from trusted Pi context to Kernel Run ownership and exact generation/root. Resume of the same saved session after restart remains the same research scope, even with a new process/sessionManager. Another logical session, including new/forked/cloned sessions, does not inherit it from copied history or paths. Actor text, a supplied owner-id DTO, a session name/Telegram thread or a matching hash is not that binding.
- **Root-first priority:** Start with the owned root Run and its index/result; then offer relevant failed/child results and selected execution, Trace, Control, capture/log or published-session files. Use Kernel-known locations and existing admitted delivery lineage, not arbitrary actor-declared paths. A child link does not grant another session's scope. No recursive directory walk, global search or full transcript load is required.
- **Dual priority orders, adopted refinement:** Support both user-selected directions through existing inspection intent, not a new tool/schema or default route. Ordinary explicit verbose maps keep independent root groups latest-registration-first; known failed descendant subtrees precede other siblings inside each root. Existing status=failed verbose investigation additionally puts failure-containing root subtrees first. Stable peer recency breaks ties and every ancestor remains before its descendants. Inventory filtering never filters navigation or rewrites catalog order. Reuse only already observed failed inventory identities matching the independently admitted map's full owner/run/generation/directory; unknown, missing, foreign or unmatched observations remain unranked rather than certified successful. Propagate priority only through available exact same-session ancestors. Report priority.root_order=latest_registration|failed_subtrees_first and priority.basis=observed_failed_rows, not complete/current terminal or membership truth. No additional status/body/native observations, callbacks, catalog repair, new registry or default/automatic map activation are authorized for ranking.
- **Ordinary reads:** Same-session permission is sufficient for investigating those owned files. Missing original producer/publication keys, producer exit or a replaced manager do not block fresh detached or retained/legacy-file research in that same logical session. Do not require a per-file capability registry, custom role/range reader or mandatory derived export for every ordinary file read; use ordinary tool offset/range support.
- **Honest navigation:** Preserve exact usable locations and containment/symlink checks; show pending/missing/unavailable/compacted evidence and known format/size/coverage honestly. The explicit map labels file_inventory_scope=canonical_starting_locations and per-file format_hint=json/jsonl/text, including missing/unavailable locations. These are layout hints, never syntax/UTF-8 validation or complete primary-output proof, and shown/omitted/total count Run rows rather than all files. Unknown/foreign ownership cannot be repaired from a path/hash/label. Deliver compact starting points and prioritized links, not all bodies. A later file read is a current observation, not a frozen multi-file snapshot.
- **No hidden effects or transfer:** Map/read operations must not export/refresh/repair evidence, re-admit a producer, probe native liveness, start work, dispatch Control, reconstruct/sample timing or ACK delivery. New navigation deliveries recheck the actual current logical session. Preserve accepted history and immutable packet/acceptance semantics; do not replay them into another session.
- **Separate stronger claims:** Reading a raw file does not certify original privacy, primary completeness, historical issuer, terminal truth or native closure. Existing curated automatic previews, publishers and private five-role verifiers retain their original-data/privacy/binding contracts, but are not the access gateway for this research route. No global legacy fail-closed, durable original-admission recovery or mandatory original-capability handoff is adopted.
- **Scope:** This is navigation/read permission, not an OS filesystem sandbox, public sharing, work/native authority, clock continuity, controller takeover or automatic Run resumption. Existing user/tool filesystem permissions are unchanged. The original sessionManager epoch remains part of its live supervision contract, not logical-session research ownership.

This decision supersedes the former original ordinary-read/lifetime gate for file investigation. The explicit manual subset below has source/npm-packed SDK tool-adapter restart/read evidence using both controlled metadata and genuine producer-exit/new-process research. The explicit-manual detail/priority subset has representative acceptance; the approved compact/outcome request handoff below extends discovery of that same existing route, not original-capability recovery or an open-ended fixture matrix. **A2 status correction after owner feedback:** a request-only inspect command is a useful implemented discovery aid, not completion of integrated file-navigation handoff. The subsequent actual-reference implementation and joint source/npm SDK evidence below now satisfy A2's representative navigation exit: exact-generation launch/terminal locations, current-recipient/session admission, shared-budget omission and ordinary selective reads. This closes navigation only, not snapshot/timing, stronger curated publication/privacy, native authority or the release. Preserve the request-only feature and legacy text stability; do not promote its green tests to whole-A2 or release acceptance. No snapshot/reporting, clock/native authority or release activation follows.

#### Shared Run-directory Catalog

**Accepted refinement; shared writer and explicit verbose read-side navigation implemented.** Reuse the existing `<configured-run-root>/index.json` and its `runs-index.ts` owner (default `~/.pi/agent/tmp/pi-actors/runs/index.json`). Do not introduce a second registry or independently written mirror. This shared catalog locates Run directories; each Run's `context-index.json` separately maps its semantic evidence roles.

- **Logical contents:** Logical Pi session → concrete Run → ordered unique real directory entries, each with `state_dir` and `run_instance_id`. Use actual Kernel-admitted Run locations/generations, including already supported explicit state directories, not arbitrary artifact paths or unpublished prepare-only state. The compatible flat entries representation is retained; private registration is implemented without a new public tool/schema. Explicit runtime/runs verbose navigation selects one trusted session branch; common launch/compatible-attachment registration is wired, while automatic map handoffs remain open.
- **Ordered-set semantics:** The most recently registered directory is first. Re-registration updates/moves its one entry rather than duplicating the directory; reuse updates the actual generation rather than retaining two historical results at one overwritten location. Reuse existing admitted path-identity rules, not a new alias/normalization scheme. Reads and status observations do not touch registration order. Order follows serialized successful registrations, not wall clocks, progress timestamps or FIFO lock-wait fairness. Recency does not override root-first investigation.
- **Concurrent publication:** Every cooperative writer of this file, including rebuild/maintenance, participates in the same destination-path lock. Acquire → reread current JSON under lock → merge the affected session/Run registration while preserving unrelated data → atomic replace through a sibling temporary file → release. Never publish a stale in-memory whole-file/session snapshot. Reuse existing file-state lock/atomic-write primitives; keep the critical section to catalog I/O, never awaited work/model calls. This must also preserve different Run updates if two processes happen to use the same logical session.
- **Failure boundaries:** Locking prevents cooperative lost updates; atomic replacement prevents readers seeing half-written JSON. Do not turn corrupt/unreadable existing data into an empty catalog and erase other sessions. A genuinely absent first catalog may be initialized; failed reads/writes/releases retain their actual errors/committed effects. Catalog publication is separate from Run launch/result truth: no rollback, rerun or invented terminal result follows from index failure. No fsync/power-loss, multi-file transaction or safety against writers bypassing the lock is claimed.
- **Session-only projection:** Kernel reads the shared catalog and issues only the actual current logical session's eligible branch, rechecking existing ownership and exact Run/generation/root. Do not advertise the whole multi-session JSON as the agent's starting map. Keep compact output/omission truth, existing lineage and ordinary same-session file access; catalog rows themselves grant neither another session's scope nor work/native authority. Ordinary navigation remains readonly, without the legacy discovery/rebuild-on-read fallback.
- **No layout/history decision:** This contract does not move files, introduce session/generation subdirectories, change Run-id namespace/defaults or bypass existing lifecycle/replacement serialization. A shared JSON lock does not solve two Runs addressing the same physical directory. Register what really exists and refuse stale/mismatched observations; multiple retained generations require actually retained distinct directories. Latest-first is not automatic rotation, eviction, deletion or a promise of history that was overwritten or never saved.

**Implemented observed-failure ordering boundary.** runs-index.ts remains the sole topology/projection owner. Its optional RunNavigationPriority is internal ordering data, not an ownership/producer capability: readonly root_order and failed identity rows. **Implemented private writer boundary.** registerRunStateDirectory in runs-index.ts admits the actual directory and exact Run/generation/optional owner through the existing bounded readRunContextIdentity observation, not a producer capability or String-coerced status DTO. Counts describe one accepted invocation, not sums across red/focused/final runs. This proves genuine Pi-recorded source investigation, not curated original privacy, authenticated membership, exhaustive closure or automatic publication.

**Earlier approved request-only navigation handoff (owner decision, 2026-10-25).** This remains the discovery aid and older formatting version; the actual-reference implementation and A2 exit above supersede treating this hint alone as completion. Compact spawn/async-tool receipts and newly admitted completion batches offer one optional current-session file-map command: inspect target=runtime view=runs verbose=true. This is the adopted minimal handoff, not an automatically embedded path map or a new mandatory reader. Existing expert launch next_actions remain and gain the same navigation request. A shared pure formatter emits compiler-owned text only; it reads no Run files, catalog, clock or Pi context and cannot grant another logical session's scope. Actual invocation remains the existing Kernel route, which rechecks the live logical session and exact admitted roots/generations. The request is not routine completion/status polling and does not auto-run. New completion batches record navigation_handoff=current_session_request as formatting data only, with strict decoding; legacy absence stays absent across pending/queued/presented reload and phases so prior accepted/proposed message text is not retrofitted or replayed. In those retained handoff packets, the one per-batch request consumes their original shared16KiB render budget and inventory/omission selection; no separate allowance, member-path disclosure or capability reconstruction is introduced. Current explicit-manual result/capture/recorded-session/published-preview, partial coverage/Unicode, containment/source-loss, dual-order/whole-row omission and restart/replacement/isolation evidence remains separate. Reference-only navigation needs no elapsed time/native authority; snapshot/status/timed joins still retain A1/B1's clock/lifetime gates, enforcement retains C and release retains D. Full-map embedding, body publication, periodic reporting and default snapshot switching are not adopted by this handoff.

#### Existing Derived Role Exports

The existing context-index design publishes `context-index.json` as a generation-fenced map of semantic evidence roles. A compact snapshot carries its pointer rather than every body. The derived schema below is useful for curated views, not the mandatory access gateway for all ordinary same-session research. Reuse existing index/location owners without silently redefining fixed export slots or adding a parallel capability registry.

Use deterministic role names and exported paths; materialize files only when their evidence exists:

```text
<run-state>/context-index.json
<run-state>/evidence/recipe.json       captured redacted Recipe/launch policy
<run-state>/evidence/result.json       semantic outcome, errors, source references
<run-state>/evidence/trace.jsonl       retained redacted observations
<run-state>/evidence/control.json      readiness, capacity, redacted Control outcomes
<run-state>/evidence/execution.json    stages, failures, captures, session provenance
<run-state>/evidence/artifacts.json    named authorized artifact references
<run-state>/evidence/sessions.json     index of readable owned-session exports
<run-state>/evidence/stdout.log        authorized retained stream view
<run-state>/evidence/stderr.log        authorized retained stream view
```

These are derived readable exports, not another canonical state store. Keep canonical files in place and reuse their owning projection/capture logic. Curated automatic Control/session/stream projections must not attach raw journals, private provider/session bodies or unredacted captures as a shortcut; published session previews still cover authorized visible turns, not hidden provider reasoning. That projection boundary does not make derived exports or producer-backed verifiers prerequisites for ordinary same-session file research. The sessions/artifacts indexes select exact sources without automatically opening bodies; declarations alone do not authorize arbitrary artifact targets.

Index shape, with two illustrative entries shown below; the actual map includes all listed roles with explicit availability:

```json
{
  "schema": "run-context-index-v1",
  "run": "review",
  "run_instance_id": "<opaque generation>",
  "updated_at": "<ISO timestamp>",
  "entries": {
    "result": { "availability": "pending", "path": null },
    "trace": {
      "availability": "available",
      "path": "evidence/trace.jsonl",
      "media_type": "application/x-ndjson",
      "bytes_total": 2048,
      "mutable": true,
      "coverage": { "truncated": false, "history_complete": false, "redacted": true }
    }
  }
}
```

Each role uses `availability: available | pending | not_applicable | unavailable`. `path` is null unless available; unavailable evidence includes a bounded reason code. An available entry has a verified regular-file path relative to this index's directory, a media type, nonnegative exported-file byte size, and explicit mutability. Reject path escapes and symlinks. Coverage describes export omissions/redaction and, for temporal evidence, whether history is complete; lost or unknown coverage cannot masquerade as complete history. Byte size refers to the readable export, not an undisclosed original. An available file is not automatically an immutable historical capture; embedded result excerpts still require the stronger source contract below.

The private [`readRunContextIndex`](../lib/runs-context-index.ts) admits index metadata for an exact owned Run/generation/optional owner. It caps marker/index metadata at 16 KiB each and canonical `run.json` input independently at 1 MiB, requires all nine role slots, validates deterministic export paths/formats/coverage, opens available regular exports only for readability/size metadata, and rejects symlinks and observed identity/view changes. It never reads role bodies, scans directories, probes processes or creates views. Pending roles need no empty files. A role size mismatch, including mutable-view drift, conservatively makes this observation unavailable until metadata is refreshed. These checks are not a multi-file atomic snapshot, proof of immutable contents or certification of declared redaction/history; the export owner still owns those facts. Returned `local_path` metadata does not itself grant exposure permission. Planned research navigation obtains it from actual logical-session/Run ownership, not an original producer key or mere presence of this field. Broader runtime navigation and public role/inspection routes remain unwired; narrow artifact/primary/diagnostic integrations do not imply a complete root-first map.

**A2 implementation inventory and privacy boundary.** The nine-role schema is not nine production publishers. Private writer/index primitives exist for Control, Recipe, Trace, Result, Execution, artifact metadata and explicitly selected session previews. Outside their owner definitions/re-exports, the maintained lib/scripts call-site inspection finds no direct ordinary calls to the Control/Recipe/Trace/Result/Execution/session Context writer wrappers. The async runner does invoke admission-bound artifact preparation before work and primary/diagnostic first-result binding, but the latter sources do not automatically publish Result role views/indexes. No generic role-refresh registry or controller-after-loss authority is implemented by those narrow integrations.

Canonical stdout/stderr have bounded strict-UTF8 kernel input and metadata-reference readers, not authorized model-facing role publishers. readRunStreamSource explicitly reports canonical_runner_stream, kernel_only and capture completeness not_asserted; those labels describe its result, not a portable visibility capability. No derived stdout/stderr export writer or original model-visibility admission was found in this closure. Neither a readable path, formatted runner output nor applying a redactor can authorize a merged/provider/session stream. The separately selected successful bare-command primary source has its own narrower original-attempt permission and cannot silently stand in for either canonical runner-stream role.

Curated saved-source verification remains role-specific: retained primary text, declaration inventories, diagnostic fields/ranges and published assistant-session previews have dedicated admission. Execution capture/session selection is a recorded-reference metadata boundary, not an Execution-role verifier. Recipe, Trace, Control, Execution and Result have the producer-backed whole-view verification below; expert Inspector projections are not those verifiers. Session preview publication still requires explicit recorded selection, never discovery. These stronger claims are separate from same-session navigation plus ordinary file tools; A2 no longer requires custom per-role range APIs or controller-owned producer-capability recovery merely to investigate an owned file.

A disposable one-producer/no-workload inquiry exposed a prerequisite defect in the then-current private Recipe publisher. writeRunRecipeContextExport read live run.json through captureRecipe/readContextMetadata for projectRunRecipe even though readRunProducerCapturedMetadata retained the original admission snapshot. Initially a known Recipe-origin literal was withheld in the view. Changing only live same-generation values.skill_dir left the original template unchanged but caused the next Recipe view to disclose that literal; the index stayed available and continued advertising the role. The captured producer snapshot still pinned the original origin. The parent captured the exact producer PID/frame before awaiting and freshly proved ESRCH immediately before deleting scratch state. This is a private publisher counterexample, not evidence about public-route behavior, a general same-UID sandbox test or proof that all role families drift.

The result establishes why available/redacted index fields and stable generation are not privacy certificates. The private Recipe correction now fixes definition/launch/privacy/stored policy to the original genuine producer capture, with no fallback to later healthy metadata. Current canonical metadata still owes identity, source-size and observation fences, not renewed selection/privacy authority. Trace and Execution have independent in-process drift regressions as well: live privacy edits previously unmasked recorded origins despite available/redacted role metadata. Both private exporters now use original Recipe privacy while their journals/manifests remain current; these tests do not prove public routes, authenticated issuers or scope closure. The separate selected-session inquiry below confirms raw-input/writer privacy drift despite surviving original producer capture; the saved-preview follower does not refresh privacy and cannot certify the writer's original redaction. The private writer now has its own original-capture correction below; raw three-argument original-privacy support remains unestablished and saved-follow semantics stay separate. Existing immutable primary/diagnostic/artifact-inventory captures remain a distinct implemented path. High-level writer wrappers reacquire producer evidence admission around capture/publication/index commits: future composition must not invoke them recursively under an already-held lifecycle lock or turn ordinary evidence I/O into an invented workload failure.

Private [`writeRunControlContextExport`](../lib/runs-context-exports.ts) now materializes `evidence/control.json` from the real canonical Control journal through original producer privacy and the existing bounded `control-projection.ts` owner. It requires the genuine runtime producer before capture and again before publication; copied/serialized producer data cannot authorize it. The JSON view has `schema: "run-context-control-v1"`, exact Run/generation/capture time, captured logical Run status, current-generation capacity, at most 40 recent projected records and explicit omitted-row/foreign-generation counts, within 64 KiB. Body and returned role metadata conservatively disclose `truncated: true`, `history_complete: false`, and `redacted: true`; retained-journal completeness is not invented. Missing/invalid source creates no pretend empty view. Existing views require the same schema/generation binding, and observed root/target changes reject atomic replacement. Write errors preserve their cause and any already-committed effects; only a later explicit call captures fresh evidence. Root results/clocks/journals remain unchanged, including while stopping or after natural acceptance. This one-file producer does not itself assemble the index, publish other roles, grant native authority, or make inspection create exports.

Private `writeRunControlContextIndex` now joins that Control view with all nine declared role slots through `writeRunContextIndexUnderLock`. It reacquires genuine producer evidence admission after view creation; the low-level writer is trusted persistence, not another authorization port. Absent Control source becomes pending metadata without an empty view; invalid/unreadable source becomes explicit unavailability while retaining previous files. New indexes mark unpublished roles unavailable. Existing same-generation role metadata is preserved, but unreadable or size-changed exports become unavailable instead of advertised paths. Corrupt, foreign-generation and nonregular index targets are preserved and refused. The writer bounds the complete index and rechecks owner, index target and admitted references before atomic replacement. View and index commits are separate: failures retain their actual partial effects, never roll back or retry the observation internally, and later explicit calls capture fresh evidence. Index metadata still does not certify body redaction, immutable history, scope closure or model exposure permission. Remaining role owners, ordinary/controller refresh and public inspection remain open.

**Control original-privacy and source boundary assessment.** A source-only one-producer/no-workload inquiry now distinguishes generic Control masking from original Recipe privacy. The pre-correction private writer required a genuine producer but captureControl/projectRunControl never consumed its captured Recipe metadata. The same inquiry uses the actual INSPECTOR_BODY_PREVIEW_CHARS limit (320): a known complete origin spanning that error boundary becomes an unrecognizable partial prefix after generic clipping. Applying original privacy afterward leaves the prefix unchanged, while whole-field privacy before the existing bounded projection produces different protected data. The correction therefore belongs before input/error clipping, not in an index flag, saved-reader repair or second pass over the clipped view. A controlled own-journal append during temporary view preparation also makes the current observation boundary explicit. Neither assessment attempt changed product code/tests/dist, created a root result, delegated work or established source-packed/public/ordinary privacy support.

Three additional owning properties cover original present/absent × caller/live before-first/refresh drift, whole-field privacy at the actual split-origin limit, unchanged generic Inspector projection, independent original/current exact UTF8 budgets and zero journal opens on original refusal, metadata/marker/copied-root replacement, and earlier-snapshot versus fresh-journal counts. Private `writeRunRecipeContextExport` materializes `evidence/recipe.json` (`run-context-recipe-v1`) from the bounded original genuine producer-admission snapshot, never from later same-generation selection/privacy or by reopening live Recipe/source files. **Private Control whole-reference correction.** The generic Inspector projector's 160-character text policy previously also shortened private context-row ids/generations. A genuine producer/property demonstrates that unsafe origin/secret ids were kept as rewritten/preview references and safe long ids/generations were not literal full references. captureControl now checks full header run/generation against the original unbounded privacy redactor before opening the journal; any privacy change yields control_identity_redacted without a fabricated masked header or source read. For each selected current-generation row, it similarly checks the complete id/generation pair before projection. A privacy-changed id omits the entire row; raw private values are never restored from a masked preview. The 40-record source selection window, foreign-generation count and raw canonical capacity remain independent of visible safe rows. controls_omitted includes privacy omissions, window omissions and any complete rows removed to satisfy the actual UTF8 encoded 64 KiB view ceiling. Metadata/root fences, recorded-snapshot source semantics, exact I/O/partial effects and no-root-result behavior remain unchanged.

Three new owning properties cover long literal Unicode/BOM/U+FFFD/backtick/CRLF identifiers plus complete generation, original-origin and generic secret-sensitive row omissions across live metadata edits, exact counts/capacity/foreign omission with byte-preserved canonical storage, large multi-row and single-row whole-reference overflow, independent owned-identity refusal, and zero journal opens for a privacy-changed header. The existing source/packed context hosts now assert full per-row generation instead of relying only on the full header. Those projection/fixture proofs alone supplied no saved reader, dispatch authority, native enforcement, ordinary/restart transport or public integration; the separate historical reader below does not widen those live authorities.

**Private historical Control whole-view admission.** writeRunControlContextExport now retains the bounded originally admitted canonical JSON and owned root in its own Control-only WeakMap, keyed by the genuine original producer after successful view publication returns (including lifecycle release). Missing/unavailable capture or a thrown before/after-replacement write/release error mints no new binding; prior admitted data remains only if the file still matches it. Explicit successful publication may replace that role's binding. Index publication is a separate effect: even a later index error does not undo a committed view/binding. Private readRunControlContextView(stateDir, producer) requires both the bounded original runtime producer and its original successful Control publication before probing for rejected admission. No Control journal, root outcome, private session/capture/other-role body, target, native/time or directory scan is read, and no work/evidence lifecycle lock, repair/export, dispatch, ACK or scheduling effect is admitted.

The saved status/readiness/capacity/pending counts describe their earlier capture, not fresh Control admission or dispatch permission: queued→claimed→failed journal changes, append or journal loss leave an otherwise matching historical view readable until explicit successful republishing. Live privacy metadata cannot reproject the original view. Seven role-specific properties prove these semantics, same-size input/id/status/capacity tampering refusal under an available index, exact long safe ids and privacy omissions, missing/copied/getter/foreign/alias/reloaded/over-budget admission before probes, before/after-replacement and separate index partial effects, selected-body-only/no-effect reads, post-body owner/root/marker/metadata/index/view substitution, corrupt/denied/symlink/failed-close refusal and independent response/source limits. Existing Recipe/Trace properties remain distinct and green.

The same source/packed context hosts now also read original frozen Control publication, reject tampering and selected-view loss without a retained-data fallback, refuse unrecorded/copied/foreign/reloaded keys, follow historical counts through later journal append/loss, and observe new counts only after explicit publication. Their complete original-generation/reference checks and exact PID/frame/stdio/close/fresh-ESRCH cleanup remain intact with no new hosts/workloads. This is private same-runtime admitted-data correspondence, not physical file-issuer authentication, ordinary/producer-loss/restart transport, public SDK/tool routing, range permission, native closure or Control actuation authority.

**Saved Recipe admission and private correspondence reader.** The existing export is bounded JSON with schema/run/generation/capture-time/coverage, capture kind, normalized recipe (composition, optional definition, diagnostics, identity, launch) and optional stored clock. This proves correspondence to allowed original projection, not who physically issued the file or durable immutability. No ordinary caller, producer-loss/restart recovery, range scheme or public route is established here. Existing primary/diagnostic source-specific canonical bindings and the saved-session reader's selected-body scope remain distinct, not blanket body permission for Recipe. The inquiry uses one self-bound child producer and zero workloads/public routes; parent captures the exact PID/frame before awaiting and freshly proves ESRCH immediately before deleting its successful scratch root. The source-only producer witness now invokes the private reader: original data succeeds, the same-sized edited body is view_mismatch despite a fresh available index, the prior readonly fence refuses, and the modified file remains unaltered. Seven owning properties cover original/copy/foreign/budget refusal before probing, live-metadata non-renewal and legacy clock absence, literals/frozen data and key-order equivalence, whole-body/policy mismatch, selected-body-only/no-effect reads, post-body root/marker/metadata/index/view substitution, malformed/denied/symlink and close-error refusal, and source/whole-response bounds. **Saved Result authority assessment — partial bindings are not whole-view correspondence.** A source-only privileged same-runtime inquiry published each original root result and Result view/index once, using five upfront controlled metadata frames/keys in one host: natural 0/7, request-only post-stop capture 0/7, and natural 7 without diagnostic fields. These are controlled canonical records and stop requests, not actual command exits or native actuation/closure. There was no re-export or reader repair; padding is JSON whitespace, not changed source bytes or relaxed index size validation.

Fresh index metadata remained available while independent changes to captured_at, outcome.recorded_at, reported flag values, a nonzero code and the natural/capture_only class continued to allow diagnostic field reads whenever fields existed. The changed saved acceptance label was returned as recorded_acceptance even when the unchanged canonical result and separate eligible-outcome reader retained the opposite classification. No-field views correctly stayed diagnostics_unavailable, which plainly cannot verify their complete metadata. The separation is intentional: readRunResultDiagnosticsSource admits the complete allowlisted retained source plus all preview/count/reference correspondence, not canonical root or arbitrary whole-view data. readRunResultDiagnosticOutcome independently admits eligible natural canonical first-result diagnostic metadata without reading view/index. Neither result, nor their conjunction, proves the historical entire saved view. The assessment therefore required Result's own original complete-data/successful-publication basis and independent field privacy, not an inferred binding from partial hashes or a different role. That assessment implemented no reader/map/carrier; the private historical admission below now supplies the separate basis without changing either partial reader. The original typed fact/reference risks identified by source inspection now have the narrower context-only publication guard below, not a promoted whole-view reader. Existing selective recovery, root 0/7/capture truth and the separate DTO-only lifetime/emission contract gate remain unchanged. The genuine shared complete-text assessment below now confirms the opaque-scalar gap in capturedTextPrivacy; selected-role body guards do not automatically certify the eligible primary/diagnostic source pipeline. Correct metadata correspondence and private fact/ref refusal remain distinct from this newly falsified complete-text privacy claim.

**Original Result context fact/reference privacy, separate from first-result bindings.** captureResult now reuses one fixed original complete metadata matcher for the full header, canonical completedAt copied into recorded_at, and actually emitted diagnostic source path/generation/hash. A private recorded fact refuses result_metadata_redacted after canonical schema/current-frame validation but before diagnostic projection. No root result or new host/workload is introduced; no parent capability reconstruction, public SDK/tool route, ordinary/restart body transport, historical file-issuer authentication, range or native closure proof is established.

Private `writeRunTraceContextExport` writes `evidence/trace.jsonl` from one admitted canonical-journal read, not the unified Trace merger. Its first JSONL record has `schema: "run-context-trace-v1"`, exact Run/generation/capture time, `source_scope: "canonical_journal"`, newest-first order, conservative partial/redacted coverage, journal summary, known read-event omissions and exact shown/omitted preview-row counts. At most 40 projected rows follow; the complete file stays within 64 KiB by dropping oldest preview rows, never slicing JSON. `projectRunTraceJournal` is a data-only port over that same parsed snapshot, preserving source IDs/order and shared summary/detail secret redaction; Recipe provenance supplies the existing runtime-origin privacy rules from the genuine original producer-admission snapshot, not later metadata or caller-object changes. This complete privacy input is admitted before opening the journal; over-budget original metadata yields trace_admission_unavailable without reading a journal body or borrowing healthy live metadata. Missing/copied producer admission remains rejected by the existing runtime-local guard. Original privacy presence or absence stays fixed, not renewed on first export/refresh, while event contents and counts follow the current admitted journal. The independent current metadata budget accepts an exact 1 MiB source, refuses one extra byte or malformed UTF-8, and never replaces prior view bytes on refusal. The captured ownership/root/marker/metadata and journal stamps remain fenced through view commit; observed same-generation substitutions refuse retargeting without internal recapture. These are observations, not immutable storage or exhaustive filesystem-race immunity. It does not read Control, process streams, execution, artifacts or private session bodies. Missing source stays pending without an invented file; malformed/incomplete/oversized/unreadable or observed-changing source stays unavailable. The source journal is capped at its existing 4 MiB bound, while privacy metadata retains the independent 1 MiB observation bound after producer admission. Journal compaction, bounded-reader event omissions and view omissions are separate evidence; diagnostics/read omissions never mean complete history. A generation-bound view is an owned-state observation, not authenticated per-event spawner membership, whole-scope closure or native authority. `writeRunTraceContextIndex` joins the actual JSONL role through the same separate producer-admitted index commit, preserving canonical storage, accepted natural results, partial effects and exact write failures. No ordinary refresh or public routing is wired.

**Saved Trace authority/format assessment.** A disposable source-only genuine-producer inquiry materializes two canonical events and their real JSONL view/index, then independently edits only one own-view summary. Both a known-original-origin edit and a privacy-safe invented summary preserve the exact encoded size, full header/capture/coverage and every other item field. The owned metadata index remains identical and available while reading zero selected-role body bytes; canonical metadata/journal/index bytes stay unchanged. Original Recipe privacy can detect the known-origin violation, but the privacy-safe forged summary is already a redaction fixed point. Both fail complete correspondence to the original-privacy projection of the unchanged canonical journal. Thus neither available/redacted metadata, schema/format checks nor reapplying original privacy alone establish saved event/header correspondence. The pure projection is also lossy: changing a private password to another same-sized private value yields identical projected items; sanitized DTOs cannot recover original source or issuer authority.

After explicit fixture restoration, a genuine third journal event leaves the earlier two-event view and index unchanged/available. Reprojecting the entire latest journal now disagrees with that authentic earlier view; an explicit writer refresh produces three matching rows. Latest-journal correspondence is therefore a possible distinct fresh-source check, not a verifier of arbitrary historical saved snapshots. It must not label the earlier publication forged, silently export/repair it during a read, or present current counts as its captured history. The existing original producer metadata pins Recipe privacy, not the dynamic journal contents/summary at every past publication.

The narrow historical path identified by this assessment is to retain the original producer's admitted Trace publication projection in the existing role owner only after successful view commit, then compare the complete selected saved header/items to that runtime-local original data. Failed/uncertain publication must mint no new binding, even if a file was replaced before an error; no later index, readable view, path/hash or DTO can reconstruct it. This is planned same-runtime admitted-data correspondence, not authentication of who physically wrote the file, an ordinary/restart transport or a generic registry. No Trace publication binding or saved reader is implemented by this assessment. Reader result/query/range schema remains unchosen; the existing 64 KiB JSONL view bound, independent 4 MiB journal writer-input bound and 1 MiB original/current metadata bounds do not themselves implement or authorize a reader response. A later whole-response bound must apply independently, without clipping rows or identities.

One successful source host/zero workloads performed the inquiry; the parent captured positive exact PID/frame before assertions/await, the child retained its frame before kernel imports, stdout/stderr/actual close were persisted before failure normalization, and fresh parent ESRCH immediately preceded successful scratch deletion. Only controlled own view edits and normal journal APIs were used; no target/session/Control/execution/stream body, root result, work delegation, new kernel interface or public/source-packed route was exercised. Existing genuine key copy refusal remains intact. This is evidence for the private next slice, not a new clock/native/ordinary read authority gate being crossed.

**Private publication-bound saved Trace verification.** writeRunTraceContextExport now retains its already admitted canonical JSONL string and owned root in a per-Trace WeakMap keyed only by the genuine original producer, after publishContextView returns successfully. Capture unavailability or a thrown write/lifecycle-release error mints no new binding, including errors after physical replacement. A prior binding remains only for its original data; an explicit later successful publication may replace it. View and index are separate effects: a later index failure does not undo the committed view or its admitted binding. No binding is persisted, serialized, reconstructed from a path/hash/flag, keyed by actor id, or shared across producer objects/modules/restart.

Neither reader probes native/time, reads root outcomes, discovers sources, opens another role body, acquires work/evidence lifecycle locks, exports/repairs, ACKs or schedules. These are cooperative runtime-local admitted-data comparisons, not authenticated physical file issuance, durable immutability or exhaustive race immunity.

Eight Trace properties cover historical frozen data after append/loss/live policy change, original optional-owner absence, missing/copied/foreign/alias/reloaded-module/original-budget admission before probing, both same-size edits and full header correspondence, failed pre/post-replacement writes and separate committed index errors, selected-body-only/no-effect reads, post-body root/marker/metadata/index/view substitution, corrupt/denied/symlink/failed-close refusal, and exact 64 KiB source versus independently oversized response/one-byte overflow. Private `writeRunResultContextExport` now materializes `evidence/result.json` (`run-context-result-v1`) as explicitly limited `content_scope: "canonical_outcome_metadata"` with `primary_text: "not_included"`. Seven owning property families partition original absolute-origin/secret run/generation/owner, captured literal/JSON-escape identity with canonical code0/7, legacy context projection and independent index refusal, safe/absent/logical/newly-added-live-origin identities, immutable first unavailability and untouched source/view orphans with zero selected-source probes, copied/getter/missing/original-budget failures, and capture-only nonpromotion. **Source authority, observation, recipient lifetime — pre-descriptor-repair assessment.** Three separate obligations meet at ordinary delivery. - Ordinary eligible diagnostic collection takes identity/status DTOs and current owned result/source data through readRunResultDiagnosticOutcome. A fresh source-only witness on the current post-captured_at candidate narrows the issue beyond retained legacy cases. The parent retains exact positive PID/Run/generation/owner/state/full upfront child frames before assertions/await, then actual saved identities/full stdio/close before normalization. **First-result diagnostic descriptor repair, not transport-authority adoption.** After explicit scoped implementation authorization, createRunResultDiagnosticsPublisher now retains its complete original pure matcher next to its original text redactor. This implements only the existing original-issuer exact-variable-reference rule. Older curated sources and packet replay remain outside its original-privacy proof. **Owner decision supersedes the ordinary read/lifetime gate.** Missing original source/producer/publication keys do not make owned Run files unreadable to their actual owning logical Pi session, including fresh detached sources and retained/legacy files after resume. **Remaining source identity assessment and captured-literal gap.** A source-only inquiry uses one captured private host with nine predeclared controlled metadata frames: baseline, original absolute-origin and generic-secret generation, original private owner, safe logical/absent owner, and generation/owner equal to a non-token-prefixed literal credential captured under values.api_key. Genuine runtime producers and each owning API are used, not copied DTO admission. The primary witness is privileged kernel selection of one synthetic full-memory command/attempt whose complete file exactly matches its in-memory text, not an executor/native workload or ordinary actor publication permission. Selected sessions are explicitly recorded synthetic Pi v3 assistant previews, not a live SDK session. Primary publication correctly projects the full selected text before preview/counts, removing Recipe origins, token-pattern secrets and the captured literal credential. The original selected-session writer correctly withholds origin/token-sensitive assistant text and user/thinking/provider data, yet publishes raw private generation in both catalog and selected preview headers; the saved-preview reader returns private owner/generation. Refresh after changing only live Recipe origin keeps the originally projected assistant body unchanged. The standalone raw three-argument preview then differs because its already documented per-call privacy changed; that distinction is not a new writer body leak or an adoption of original privacy in the raw API. Safe logical-suffix and absent-owner cases remain available under their respective current contracts.

The extra literal cases expose a narrower root defect in the earlier shared identity comparison. A credential value captured under a sensitive key is masked by primary text privacy and withheld from an artifact path, but not by Recipe-origin/generic-pattern identity comparison when it lacks a recognizable token assignment. It therefore remains in primary/inventory generation references and selected-session headers; a comparative genuine Recipe publication/read and Result context export also accept and echo that literal in their header/reply identity. This assessment does not silently widen session body policy or the artifact operational-path exception.

That assessment itself changed no implementation/tests/distributive. It motivated the shared original identity captured-literal correction below (including Control row references); the subsequent own-boundary primary/inventory/session correction is recorded below, not supplied by that assessment. Existing source/body/byte limits, accepted result codes, first bindings and separate source/view/catalog/index effects must remain unchanged. Both native inquiry runs use immediate positive host/frame capture, complete stdout/stderr/close persistence before normalization and fresh captured-PID ESRCH immediately before successful scratch deletion; the first seven-frame positive matrix and the expanded nine-frame matrix are distinct observations, not added acceptance counts. The child never certifies its own exit. These are bounded private source counterexamples, not proof of an ordinary public leak, producer-loss authority, new visibility permission, native closure or release readiness.

**Implemented captured-literal identity repair.** The nine-frame source assessment above remains pre-repair evidence for its comparative Recipe/Result guard cases. The shared original-backed guard derives one pure createContextIdentityPrivacyCheck from bounded genuine original metadata; its matcher remains private to each admitted caller. No masked reference, source repair, new protocol/visibility flag, registry, module or producer/work permission is introduced; Result first code/acceptance/unavailability stays immutable and DTO-only old-source emission remains contract-gated.

**Readonly retained diagnostic follows.** `readRunResultDiagnosticsSource(stateDir, expected, query)` now follows this saved metadata projection without a live producer. Query is a closed own-data `{field}` or `{field,start,end}` with `field: "command" | "error" | "failure_reason"`; both byte endpoints must be supplied together as safe ordered nonnegative integers. Absent range returns the exact recorded <=4000-code-point preview, never a synthesized summary or another field. Admission checks exact owned Run/generation/optional owner, bounded canonical index, available mutable Result metadata role, regular bounded view and deterministic generation/owner diagnostic source path. It validates the complete closed view/outcome/descriptor and source schemas, Run/generation/content/privacy scope, code/time/recorded natural-versus-capture-only classification, all coverage/producer-flag shapes, entire strict-UTF8 source hash/byte length and every recorded field's well-formed scalar text/preview/byte/code-point/omission counts, including unselected fields. Legacy views, absent/unpublished indexes, `not_recorded` and foreign/malformed descriptors refuse before any retained-source follow. An available file or copied DTO is not producer/body/terminal authority. A recorded capture-only classification remains metadata and gains no terminal status or primary permission.

Results curate/deep-freeze identity, source descriptor and the selected excerpt, reporting `content_scope: "canonical_outcome_diagnostic_strings"`, `privacy_scope: "producer_admission"`, `primary_text: "not_included"`, redacted policy, selected field/range/counts and `recorded_acceptance`. The latter is saved-view metadata, not a new read of current root outcome/closure. The reader never opens raw `result.json`, runner/capture/session/artifact bodies, original Recipe paths or sibling diagnostics; it never probes processes, reprojects live privacy, reconstructs a producer, writes/exports/repairs, ACKs or takes a lifecycle/work lock. Source input remains 4 MiB, view/complete encoded response 64 KiB, index 16 KiB and owned metadata 1 MiB. Split-scalar, out-of-bounds and oversized selections refuse the whole response, not silently clip or clamp it.

Identity/marker/metadata/index/evidence/view/source observations are fenced through reading and returned `unchanged()`. Legacy `readExecutionTurns` now shares this reference boundary, avoids scanning known symlinked namespaces and discards observed-changing files after its existing body read. Previously a symlinked `sessions` root could redefine the apparent ownership boundary and expose a foreign session; the before/after fixture reproduces and blocks that case. Raw references still do not appear in the public context index or grant private-body visibility; source/packed body-export journeys and native platform acceptance are not established by these filesystem observations.

Private readonly `readRunExecutionReference` now selects one explicitly requested capture or session from an exact owned Run/generation manifest. It reuses `readRunContextIdentity` over the context-index owner, without needing an index, producer capability, process probe or lifecycle lock: only published canonical metadata with the exact optional owner is admitted; preparation alone is unavailable. Marker metadata retains its 16 KiB bound, while canonical Run and execution-manifest inputs are each independently capped at 1 MiB. The manifest uses the shared strict version/generation/command/attempt normalization. Wrong or unbound identities, malformed metadata and unknown requests cannot authorize a private-file probe. A capture must match the standard recorded command/attempt/stream path; a session must be explicitly recorded on that selected command, never discovered or inferred from `session_dir`. Only the selected regular/readable file is metadata-opened; raw capture/session bodies and directory inventories are not read, unrelated source files are not opened, and parsed unknown/provider metadata is not projected. Both Execution manifest export and reference admission select the resilient JSON reader's opt-in strict UTF-8 mode: malformed bytes cannot normalize into admitted replacement text, while a valid encoded replacement character remains valid. Default legacy JSON/JSONL degradation is unchanged. A known saved-size disagreement is unavailable, while unknown saved bytes remain null even when a current file size is observed. Source limits are observational, not workload/file quotas; exact UTF-8 metadata boundaries and large sparse captures stay independent. Root/marker/metadata, manifest and file changes invalidate the observation, including through its returned `unchanged()` callback; rejected or missing evidence is never repaired. All paths/callbacks remain kernel-local, mutable observations: not retained descriptors, immutable historical contents, privacy/body-exposure permission, native membership/closure or reconstructed producer/work authority. Legacy-unbound data gains no fresh authority. This port writes no view/index/status/ACK/clock and does not switch public inspection. Authorized stream exports, stable retained primary sources, role/range journeys and ordinary/controller refresh remain required; private session preview publication is described below.

Private readonly `readRunSessionPreview` now defines one fixed visibility policy above recorded-reference admission, not a body permission inferred from a metadata DTO. This three-argument readonly port accepts no original producer/privacy capability: fixed assistant-only content policy is not immutable producer-admission privacy across calls. Original privacy support is unestablished here; current metadata, reference DTOs and unchanged callbacks cannot reconstruct it. Metadata observation remains body-free and unbounded by file-content size; the separate text port reads at most the existing 4 MiB session-evidence input allowance through one checked regular-file descriptor, rejects known oversize before allocation/body read, strictly decodes UTF-8 and rejects observed file/root/manifest changes or failed cleanup without retries. This is a preview observation budget, never a workload/file quota. Pure `projectSessionAssistantPreview` accepts recorded Pi v3 JSONL with unambiguous ids/parent ancestry, sharing `selectLastRecordedSessionBranch` with the legacy evidence owner. It rejects malformed/unsupported/ambiguous or incomplete branch provenance rather than guessing. `source_scope: "last_recorded_branch"` means the physical last recorded leaf, not a live SessionManager selection or current model context; raw-history exports do not replay context edits. Only assistant text blocks are projected. User/system/tool/custom content, thinking, signatures, usage, diagnostics and provider payload fields are omitted; raw header/session identity and physical-reference metadata are not copied. Literal assistant strings still require correct Recipe-origin privacy: this is not a generic path-suppression rule. Per-call captured-origin/secret redaction runs before code-point preview counts and the 4,000-character per-message ceiling; default Recipe redaction remains unchanged. This ordering does not make later calls use the original launch privacy. At most 40 recent visible messages fit a separate 64 KiB encoded bound by dropping whole oldest preview rows with exact observation omission counts. Coverage remains explicitly partial/redacted/history-incomplete, even for an empty valid session. The response binds Run/generation/command, exposes no raw local path and labels `primary_text: "not_exported"`; no full authorised text is retained and no recoverable source/range is invented, so A3 is still required. This port creates no views/index/status/ACK/clock, probes no process and reconstructs no native/work authority. Ordinary producer/controller refresh, stream views and public role/range journeys remain open; private session preview publication is described below.

Private `writeRunSessionContextExport` and `writeRunSessionContextIndex` now materialize those assistant-only previews through `runs-session-exports.ts`, re-exported by the context-export facade. A genuine runtime producer is required for capture, publication and later index admission; copied producer data and malformed root-outcome evidence still refuse before publication. Over-budget original data yields session_admission_unavailable without borrowing a healthy per-call preview or opening raw sessions. Missing/copied producer admission remains rejected by the existing guard. It is not an additional exported input capability: public readRunSessionPreview remains three-argument/per-call, even if a JavaScript caller supplies an extra privacy object, and the saved follower never reprojects. Both each encoded preview and the complete catalog are capped independently at 64 KiB. Missing, denied, malformed or foreign cached peer previews become null/unavailable references without deleting their files. The original source and generation are rechecked before preview/catalog replacement; source changes reject refresh and preserve prior bytes. Preview and catalog share a synchronous evidence admission but are separate atomic-file effects; the context index uses a later producer admission and fresh catalog/reference checks. Exact before/after-commit errors escape without internal write retry or rollback. Catalog overflow refuses the whole metadata update rather than publishing partial lists, but a new preview may already have committed and remains retained; A3 must still deliver recoverable complete overflow/primary sources. The catalog/index and nested files are not one atomic snapshot, privacy certificate or immutable historical archive: later selective follows must revalidate each nested reference/body generation and coverage. These private writers preserve canonical Run/result/clocks/raw sessions, including after natural acceptance, and do not switch public inspection or claim native scope closure. Ordinary/controller refresh and public source/packed role/range journeys remain open.

Private readonly `readRunSessionContextPreview(stateDir, expected, exportId)` now follows one published session preview through that owner. It accepts only a selected 64-hex catalog id, admits the exact owned Run/generation/optional owner, verifies context-index/session-role metadata (including the fixed partial coverage and mutability), then parses the bounded strict-UTF8 catalog and selected nested file. Stored paths must match the canonical hashed layout; body Run/generation/command/export identity, capture time, byte size, allowed fields and Unicode/row omission counts must agree. Missing, unlisted, unavailable, malformed, stale, contradictory or symlinked evidence is bounded unavailability, never a newer generation, repair, automatic export or an invented empty preview. Shared read-only binding types carry just identity data, never a reconstructed producer/work capability. This read performs no writes, directory discovery, process probes, lifecycle/scheduling/ACK effects or availability refresh. It is not an atomic multi-file snapshot, immutable archive, body authenticity certificate, native closure proof or a public role/range route; A1 integration, remaining selective readers, source/packed public journeys and A3 completion/source routing remain required.

**Selected-session boundary evidence and bounded writer correction.** A source-only scratch inquiry uses one genuine self-bound child producer, one explicitly recorded synthetic Pi v3 session and zero workloads/public routes/live-registry writes. Initial raw and published previews withhold the original Recipe origin. Changing only live same-generation values.skill_dir leaves the raw session and execution manifest byte-identical but makes the next raw preview disclose the origin and changes its projected observed/omitted counts while still showing exactly 4,000 points. Before republishing, a fresh saved-preview follow returns the unchanged original preview with zero raw-session opens. Before the correction, producer republishing overwrote the same selection id with the unsafe per-call preview; catalog/index remained available/redacted and the selected saved reader returned those validated stored bytes. User/thinking/provider fields remain excluded. The old raw observation's fence refuses after metadata change, which does not supply original privacy for a fresh call. Parent-captured exact PID/frame and fresh ESRCH immediately before deletion settle only this isolated host; the child leaves its own exit unasserted. This establishes distinct private boundary behavior, not public leakage/absence, a native/sandbox proof or a new visibility permission. The corrected source-only witness repeats the same inquiry: the raw per-call preview still changes as before, but producer republishing retains exactly the original projected preview and counts under the same selection id; its saved follow no longer discloses the original origin. Fifteen owning checks include current-branch appends, effectful live privacy edits in both presence/absence cases, an ignored extra public raw-reader argument, independent original/current UTF-8 budgets and source/manifest/metadata/marker/root refusal through commit. Source/catalog/index and exact partial errors remain separate, including natural outcomes and catalog overflow. This is bounded private acceptance, not ordinary/public/source-packed role acceptance or support for original privacy on the standalone raw reader. No new readonly privacy carrier, kernel interface or default policy is adopted.

Private `readRunStreamSource(stateDir, expected, stream)` now admits one fixed canonical runner `stdout` or `stderr` as **kernel-only projection input**, not a readable/model-facing context view.
